2025-06-18T17:53:53.2532142Z     166.Force-reinstalling core wheels with `--no-deps` prevents pip from attempting to compile dependencies that fail on the OT-2's limited environment.

2025-06-18T17:53:53.2533420Z     168.## Usage Examples

2025-06-18T18:18:41.6247260Z   +log_command() {
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6247345Z   +    local cmd="$1"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6247432Z   +    local description="$2"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6247545Z   +    local timestamp=$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6247615Z   +    
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6247786Z   +    echo -e "${CYAN}[$timestamp]${NC} ${BLUE}EXECUTING:${NC} $description"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6247931Z   +    echo -e "${CYAN}[$timestamp]${NC} ${YELLOW}COMMAND:${NC} $cmd"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6248004Z   +    
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6248077Z   +    # Log to file
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6248153Z   +    {
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6248266Z   +        echo "[$timestamp] PHASE: $description"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6248363Z   +        echo "[$timestamp] COMMAND: $cmd"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6248440Z   +    } >> "$LOG_FILE"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6248506Z   +    
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6248616Z   +    # Execute command and capture all output
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6248695Z   +    local exit_code=0
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6248787Z   +    local stdout_file=$(mktemp)
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6248878Z   +    local stderr_file=$(mktemp)
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6248950Z   +    
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6249035Z   +    if [[ "$cmd" == ssh* ]]; then
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6249133Z   +        # For SSH commands, execute directly
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6249276Z   +        eval "$cmd" > "$stdout_file" 2> "$stderr_file" || exit_code=$?
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6249344Z   +    else
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6249428Z   +        # For local commands
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6249573Z   +        bash -c "$cmd" > "$stdout_file" 2> "$stderr_file" || exit_code=$?
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6249646Z   +    fi
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6249714Z   +    
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6249824Z   +    local stdout_content=$(cat "$stdout_file")
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6249924Z   +    local stderr_content=$(cat "$stderr_file")
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6250186Z   +    
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6250277Z   +    # Display results
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6250365Z   +    if [ $exit_code -eq 0 ]; then
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6250585Z   +        echo -e "${GREEN}✅ SUCCESS${NC} (exit code: $exit_code)"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6250795Z   +    else
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6250981Z   +        echo -e "${RED}❌ FAILED${NC} (exit code: $exit_code)"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6251050Z   +    fi
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6251122Z   +    
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6251215Z   +    if [ -n "$stdout_content" ]; then
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6251309Z   +        echo -e "${GREEN}STDOUT:${NC}"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6251546Z   +        echo "$stdout_content" | head -20  # Show first 20 lines
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6251667Z   +        if [ $(echo "$stdout_content" | wc -l) -gt 20 ]; then
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6251814Z   +            echo "... (output truncated, see log file for full output)"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6251882Z   +        fi
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6251958Z   +    fi
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6252024Z   +    
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6252120Z   +    if [ -n "$stderr_content" ]; then
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6252207Z   +        echo -e "${RED}STDERR:${NC}"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6252367Z   +        echo "$stderr_content" | head -10  # Show first 10 lines of errors
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6252498Z   +        if [ $(echo "$stderr_content" | wc -l) -gt 10 ]; then
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6252655Z   +            echo "... (error output truncated, see log file for full output)"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6252727Z   +        fi
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6252793Z   +    fi
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6252864Z   +    
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6252937Z   +    # Log to file
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6253010Z   +    {
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6253124Z   +        echo "[$timestamp] EXIT_CODE: $exit_code"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6253224Z   +        echo "[$timestamp] STDOUT_START"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6253311Z   +        echo "$stdout_content"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6253406Z   +        echo "[$timestamp] STDOUT_END"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6253496Z   +        echo "[$timestamp] STDERR_START"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6253580Z   +        echo "$stderr_content"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6253671Z   +        echo "[$timestamp] STDERR_END"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6253875Z   +        echo "[$timestamp] STATUS: $([ $exit_code -eq 0 ] && echo "SUCCESS" || echo "FAILED")"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6253972Z   +        echo "[$timestamp] ---"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6254043Z   +        echo ""
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6254121Z   +    } >> "$LOG_FILE"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6254188Z   +    
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6254275Z   +    # Cleanup temp files
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6254368Z   +    rm -f "$stdout_file" "$stderr_file"
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6254439Z   +    
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6254533Z   +    # Brief pause for readability
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6254607Z   +    sleep 1
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6254679Z   +    
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6254757Z   +    return $exit_code
2025-06-18T18:18:41.6254827Z   +}
